- **seo-calendar-refill**: Weekly, browser only when a source refuses to be fetched. Counts unpublished calendar entries per property by folding the published ledger against the calendar, and where every property is above its runway threshold it records the counts and exits, which is the normal run. Where a property is low it researches the next block from rank evidence, the topic map, and live result sets, refuses any keyword already targeted, and appends fully specified entries after the last existing block. It is the only writer of the calendar and it never modifies, reorders, or renumbers an existing entry.
